# Agents · R · part 15

961 entries, repo-researcher to repository-intake. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **repo-researcher**: Read-only researcher for one assigned area of a repository. Maps relevant files, callers, contracts, permission checks, existing patterns and verification commands. Use before planning. Never edits files and never runs tests. ([CJCreator-New/CJ-Creator-Skills](https://github.com/CJCreator-New/CJ-Creator-Skills/blob/HEAD/repo-orchestrator/agents/repo-researcher.md))
- **repo-researcher**: Fast read-only exploration of the codebase and docs to answer "where/how is X done" questions with file references. Use before planning a slice in an unfamiliar area, instead of loading many files into the main context. ([esterkane/audhs-tutor](https://github.com/esterkane/audhs-tutor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/repo-researcher.md))
- **repo-researcher**: Investigador de solo lectura del repositorio. Usalo para mapear arquitectura, localizar codigo, entender convenciones o reunir contexto amplio sin saturar la conversacion principal. Devuelve hallazgos concisos con rutas y citas, no cambios. ([jaisenbe58r/crewai-vision-tool](https://github.com/jaisenbe58r/crewai-vision-tool/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/repo-researcher.md))
- **repo-researcher**: Deep GitHub repository analyst. Given a repo URL, reads the ENTIRE repo — every file, README section, component, agent definition, hook, workflow, MCP config, and code path — then produces a full file inventory, architecture overview, feature gap analysis vs BrickLayer 2.0, and prioritized recommendations. Saves output to docs/repo-research/{repo-name}.md in the BrickLayer project directory. Use when Tim wants to research a GitHub repo for patterns to incorporate into BrickLayer. ([Nerfherder16/BrickLayer](https://github.com/Nerfherder16/BrickLayer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/repo-researcher.md))

- **repo-resolver**: Works out which registered repositories a ticket is built in, when the ticket's `repositories` property is empty or names a code the registry no longer has. Reads the ticket, its feature and design docs, the registry, and the candidate repositories' code, and returns the registry codes with the evidence that settled them. Use when `/orchestrate`, `/orchestrate-quick`, or `/run-roadmap` picks a ticket with no usable `repositories`, or when `/plan` cannot settle them itself. Returns an answer only — writes no file and no code. ([husdeli/skills](https://github.com/husdeli/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/sdlc/agents/repo-resolver.md))
